\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma\theta}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma)\right\|^{2}+\gamma\left(\theta+\frac{1}{2\lambda}\right)\left\|\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})-\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))\right\|^{2},

and after rearranging the terms, we finally have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma\right)\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\gamma\theta}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t+1},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t+1}))\right)

\displaystyle+\left(2+\frac{1}{\lambda\theta}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})\right\|^{2}

to finish the proof.
∎

B.2
Proof of Theorem
3.2

Theorem B.9

(Restatement of Theorem
3.2
)
.

Under Assumptions
2.1
-
2.3
, let
\alpha\leq\frac{1}{L_{g,1}}
,
M=\left\lceil-\frac{\ln T}{\ln\rho}\right\rceil
and
\gamma\leq\frac{1}{2L_{F}}
, Algorithm
1
can obtain

\displaystyle\mathrm{Reg}(T)\leq\frac{16Q}{\gamma}+6\kappa_{F}\delta^{2}T+768L_{f,0}^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}+\frac{8}{\gamma}V_{T}=O\left(\delta^{2}T+V_{T}\right),

with total number of inner iterations
\mathcal{I}_{T}
satisfies

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{T}\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{2\ln\delta}{\ln(1-\alpha\mu_{g}))}\right)T+\frac{3\delta^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}}{\ln(1-\alpha\mu_{g})^{-1}}T+\frac{3\kappa_{g}^{2}L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}(1+\kappa_{g})^{2}\gamma^{2}}{\ln(1-\alpha\mu_{g})^{-1}}T+\frac{3L_{g,1}^{2}}{\ln(1-\alpha\mu_{g})^{-1}}H_{2,T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(T\log\delta^{-1}+H_{2,T}\right).

Proof.

It begins with Lemma
B.8
, shown as

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma\right)\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\gamma\theta}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t+1},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t+1}))\right)

\displaystyle+\left(2+\frac{1}{\lambda\theta}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})\right\|^{2},

(43)

it then follows that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})+\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})\mathbf{v}_{t+1}-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))\mathbf{v}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})\right\|^{2}+2\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})\mathbf{v}_{t+1}-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))\mathbf{v}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(i)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2L_{f,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t+1}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}+4\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})\mathbf{v}_{t+1}-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})\mathbf{v}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+4\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})\mathbf{v}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))\mathbf{v}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(ii)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(2L_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{4L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,2}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t+1}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}+4L_{g,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t+1}-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}

(44)

\displaystyle\overset{(iii)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(2L_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{4L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,2}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}+\frac{16L_{f,1}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}+\frac{16L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{g,2}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{4}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t+1}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{128L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\rho^{M},

(45)

where
(i)
,
(ii)
comes from the Lipschitz continuity of
\nabla f_{t}(\cdot)
in Assumption
2.2
and Lemma
B.3
,
(iii)
comes from Lemma
B.4
with
M_{t}\equiv M
. Due to (
21
), let
\mathbf{x}=\mathbf{x}_{t}
,
\mathbf{y}_{1}=\mathbf{y}_{t+1}
and
\mathbf{y}_{2}=\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})
we have

\displaystyle\mu_{g}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t+1}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|\leq\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})\right\|\leq\delta,

which implies that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{2L_{f,1}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}+\frac{4L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,2}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{4}}+\frac{16L_{f,1}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{4}}+\frac{16L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{g,2}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{6}}\right)\delta^{2}+\frac{128L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\rho^{M},

and summing over
t\in[T]
to get

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})\right\|^{2}\leq\kappa_{F}\delta^{2}T+128L_{f,0}^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}\rho^{M}T,

(46)

where

\displaystyle\kappa_{F}:=\frac{2L_{f,1}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}+\frac{4L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,2}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{4}}+\frac{16L_{f,1}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{4}}+\frac{16L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{g,2}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{6}}.

(47)

Finally, with Lemma
B.7
and substituting (
46
) into (
43
), we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma\right)\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4Q}{\gamma\theta}+\frac{2}{\gamma\theta}V_{T}+\left(2+\frac{1}{\lambda\theta}\right)\kappa_{F}\delta^{2}T+\left(2+\frac{1}{\lambda\theta}\right)\frac{128L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\rho^{M}T,

let
M=\left\lceil-\frac{\ln T}{\ln\rho}\right\rceil
and
\gamma\leq\frac{1}{2L_{F}}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Reg}(T)\leq\frac{16Q}{\gamma}+6\kappa_{F}\delta^{2}T+768L_{f,0}^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}+\frac{8}{\gamma}V_{T}=O\left(\delta^{2}T+V_{T}\right).

Next we prove the upper bound on the total number of inner iterations. Consider the sequence
\{\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k}\}_{k=1}^{K_{t}}
generated in every
t
:

\displaystyle\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k+1}\leftarrow\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k}-\alpha\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k}).

Based on existing conclusions of gradient descent in strongly convex optimization problems, it implies that

\displaystyle\frac{\delta^{2}}{L_{g,1}^{2}}\overset{(i)}{\leq}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}^{K_{t}}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}\leq\rho^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}^{1}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2},

(48)

where
(i)
comes from the fact that Algorithm
1
maintains iteration when
\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})\right\|>\delta
and if let
\mathbf{x}=\mathbf{x}_{t}
,
\mathbf{y}_{1}=\mathbf{y}_{t}^{K_{t}}
and
\mathbf{y}_{2}=\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})
, in (
20
) we have

\displaystyle L_{g,1}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}^{K_{t}}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|\geq\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{K_{t}})\right\|>\delta,

(49)

also
\rho=1-\mu_{g}/L_{g,1}
if
\alpha\leq 1/L_{g,1}
. Then rearranging the terms of (
48
), we have

\displaystyle\rho^{K_{t}-1}\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\delta^{2}}{L_{g,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle(K_{t}-1)\ln\rho\geq

\displaystyle 2\ln\delta-\ln(L_{g,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2})

\displaystyle K_{t}\leq

\displaystyle 1-\frac{\ln(L_{g,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2})}{\ln\rho}+\frac{2\ln\delta}{\ln\rho}

\displaystyle K_{t}\leq

\displaystyle 1+\frac{L_{g,1}^{2}}{\ln\rho^{-1}}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{2\ln\delta}{\ln\rho},

thus

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}K_{t}\leq\left(1+\frac{2\ln\delta}{\ln\rho}\right)T+\frac{L_{g,1}^{2}}{\ln\rho^{-1}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}.

(50)

We also have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle 3\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}-\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})\right\|^{2}{+}3\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})-\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}{+}3\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{3\delta^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}T+3\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1},\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1},\mathbf{y}_{t},\mathbf{v}_{t}),\gamma)\right\|^{2}+3H_{2,T}

\displaystyle\overset{(i)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{3\delta^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}T+3\kappa_{g}^{2}L_{f,0}^{2}(1+\kappa_{g})^{2}\gamma^{2}T+3H_{2,T},

(51)

where
(i)
comes from the fact that

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x},\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{v}),\gamma)\right\|\leq\left\|\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{v})\right\|=\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\mathbf{v}\right\|\leq L_{f,0}+L_{g,1}\frac{L_{f,0}}{\mu_{g}}.

Finally, substituting (
51
) into (
50
), it holds that

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{T}:=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}K_{t}\leq\left(1{+}\frac{2\ln\delta}{\ln\rho}\right)T+\frac{3\delta^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}}{\ln\rho^{-1}}T+\frac{3\kappa_{g}^{2}L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}(1{+}\kappa_{g})^{2}\gamma^{2}}{\ln\rho^{-1}}T+\frac{3L_{g,1}^{2}}{\ln\rho^{-1}}H_{2,T}=O\left(T\log\delta^{-1}+H_{2,T}\right).

Thus we finish the proof.
∎

B.3
Proof of Theorem
3.3

In Section
3.1
, we assert that AOBO reaches an regret upper bound of
O(1+V_{T})
, which matches the lower bound of the proof in
[
14
]
on single-layer online nonconvex optimization and thus Theorem
3.3
naturally holds. Therefore, we believe that AOBO is optimal on OBO. Although the scenarios are different,
V_{T}
is defined to characterize the change of the objective function value over time, and OBO is a more difficult scenario than single-layer optimization, so it should have at least a larger lower bound. For the sake of rigor and completeness, we still provide the proof of Theorem
3.3
. Remind of the online hypergradient-based algorithm class defined in Definition
2.4
, which is satisfied by AOBO, OBBO
[
4
]
, SOBOW
[
25
]
, FSOBO and SOGD
[
30
]
without noise.

Definition B.10

(Restatement of Definition
2.4
)
.

Suppose there are totally
T
time steps, the iterates
\{(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
are generated according to
(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})\in\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t},\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t}
with
\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{x}}^{1}=\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{y}}^{1}=\{0\}
.

For all
t
, the linear subspace
\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t}
is allowed to expand
K
times with
\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t,1}=\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t}
for
k=1,\dots,K
, shown as:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t,k+1}\leftarrow\mathrm{Span}\big\{

\displaystyle\mathbf{y}_{i},\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{i}(\widetilde{\mathbf{x}}_{i},\widetilde{\mathbf{y}}_{i})\big\},

where
\widetilde{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\in\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{x}}^{i}
,
\mathbf{y}_{i},\widetilde{\mathbf{y}}_{i}\in\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t,j}
and
i\in[1,t]
,
j\in[1,k]
.

Then with
\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t+1}=\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t,K}
, define
S>0
,
\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}
expand as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t+1}\leftarrow\mathrm{Span}\Big\{\mathbf{x}_{i},\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f_{i}(\widetilde{\mathbf{x}}_{i},\widetilde{\mathbf{y}}_{j}),\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{i}(\overline{\mathbf{x}}_{i},\overline{\mathbf{y}}_{j})\prod_{j=1}^{s}\left(\mathbf{I}_{d_{2}}-\alpha\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{i}(\mathbf{x}_{i}^{s},\mathbf{y}_{j}^{s})\right)\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{x}}_{i},\widehat{\mathbf{y}}_{i})\Big\},\!\!

(52)

where
\mathbf{x}_{i},\widetilde{\mathbf{x}}_{i},\overline{\mathbf{x}}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{i}^{s},\widehat{\mathbf{x}}_{i}\in\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{x}}^{i}
,
\widetilde{\mathbf{y}}_{j},\overline{\mathbf{y}}_{j},\mathbf{y}_{j}^{s},\widehat{\mathbf{y}}_{j}\in\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{y}}^{j}
with any
\alpha\in\mathbb{R}
for
i\in[1,t]
,
j\in[1,t+1]
and
s\in[1,S]
.

Our proof follows the same idea as the proof of Theorem 1 in
[
14
]
. Given a total budget of
V_{T}
variation, the bilevel objective function
f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))
we constructed has
\Omega(1+V_{T})
times of change. Thus, we divide the total time steps into
\Omega(1+V_{T})
blocks, functions within each block remains the same while changing across a new block. Now, we construct a series of functions whose gradients are orthogonal to each other and assign them to these blocks, the algorithm will be forced to restart the learning process when entering a new block and hence the accumulated local regret is
\Omega(1+V_{T})
.

Theorem B.11

(Restatement of Theorem
3.3
)
.

Consider any online hypergradient-based algorithm
\mathcal{A}
that satisfies Definition
2.4
with any
K,S>0
, under Assumptions
2.1
-
2.3
with
d_{1}=d_{2}\geq\Omega(1+V_{T})
, there exist
\{f_{t},g_{t}\}_{t=1}^{T}
that satisfy
V_{T}=o(T)
for which
\mathrm{Reg}(T)\geq\Omega(1+V_{T})
.

Proof.

Assume
\mathbf{x}
and
\mathbf{y}
have the same dimension
d
. For any vector
\mathbf{a}
, We use
[\mathbf{a}]_{i}
to represent the
i
-th coordinate of
\mathbf{a}
and
[\mathbf{a}]_{i:j}
represent the coordinates from the
i
-th to the
j
-th coordinate of
\mathbf{a}
.

Step 1: Constructing the functions
f_{t}
,
g_{t}
that satisfying Assumptions
2.1
-
2.3
.

We fixedly divide the function group
\{f_{t}\}_{t=1}^{T}
into
N
blocks, and the size of each block is
B=\left\lceil T/N\right\rceil
. For
k\in[N]
, we have
f_{(k-1)B+1}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=f_{(k-1)B+2}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=\dots=f_{kB}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=\widetilde{f}_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})
, which is defined as

\displaystyle\widetilde{f}_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=c\sigma([\mathbf{y}]_{k})+c\sigma([\mathbf{x}]_{k}),\quad\text{and}\quad g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\equiv g(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=\frac{\mu}{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{x}\right\|^{2},

where
\sigma(\cdot)
denotes the sigmoid function.
For any
\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
and
\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right\|\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}c}{4},\quad\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})-\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}^{\prime})\right\|\leq\frac{\sqrt{3}c}{18}\left\|(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})-(\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}^{\prime})\right\|,

thus we set
c=\min\{\frac{Q}{2},2\sqrt{2}L_{f,0},6\sqrt{3}L_{f,1}\}
to let
f_{t}
satisfies Assumptions
2.2
and
2.3
for all
t\in[T]
.

For any
\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,
\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=\mu(\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{x})
, it follows that
\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=\mu\mathbf{I}_{d}
and
\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=-\mu\mathbf{I}_{d}
.

g_{t}(\cdot)
is
\mu_{g}
-strongly convex if
\mu=\mu_{g}
, and thus
\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right\|=\mu_{g}\leq L_{g,1}
.

\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\cdot)
and
\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})
satisfy
L_{g,2}
-Lipschitz continuity because they are all constant matrices.

Consider the exact function-value variation
V_{T}^{F}
we constructed, it follows that

\displaystyle V_{T}^{F}=\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sup_{\mathbf{x}}\left|f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x})-f_{t-1}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))\right|=\sum_{k=2}^{N}\sup_{\mathbf{x}}\left|2c\sigma([\mathbf{x}]_{k})-2c\sigma([\mathbf{x}]_{k-1})\right|\leq Q(N-1)\leq V_{T},

where the last inequality holds if set
N=\left\lceil\frac{1+V_{T}}{Q}\right\rceil
and thus
B=\left\lfloor\frac{QT}{1+V_{T}}\right\rfloor
.

Step 2: Characterizing the lower bound of bilevel local regret.

For the first block
k=1
, with

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}\widetilde{f}_{1}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=c\sigma^{\prime}([\mathbf{x}]_{1}),\quad\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widetilde{f}_{1}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=c\sigma([\mathbf{y}]_{1})

and
\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=\mu_{g}\mathbf{I}_{d}
, according to the algorithm’s subspace update rule (
52
), we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}=\dots=\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{x}}^{B+1}=\mathrm{Span}\{e_{1}\},

which means that in the first block, only the first coordinate is activated, while the rest remain at
0
, and this situation continues until it moves to the next block.
After repeating the same steps, it implies that

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{x}}^{(k-1)B+2}=\dots=\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{x}}^{kB+1}=\mathrm{Span}\{e_{1},\dots,e_{k}\}.

(53)

Now note that
\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x})=\mathbf{x}
for all
t\in[T]
, we have

\displaystyle f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))=2c\sigma([\mathbf{x}]_{k}),\quad\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))=2c\sigma^{\prime}([\mathbf{x}]_{k})e_{k}.

When in a new block,
t=(k-1)B+1
, due to the equation (
53
), it follows that

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{x}}^{(k-1)B+1}=\mathrm{Span}\{e_{1},\dots,e_{k-1}\},

thus

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))\right\|=\left\|2c\sigma^{\prime}([\mathbf{x}_{(k-1)B+1}]_{k})e_{k}\right\|=2c\sigma^{\prime}(0)=\frac{c}{2}.

Finally, we finish the proof by

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))\right\|^{2}\geq\sum_{k=1}^{N}\left\|2c\sigma^{\prime}([\mathbf{x}_{(k-1)B+1}]_{k})e_{k}\right\|^{2}=4Nc^{2}\sigma^{\prime}(0)^{2}=\frac{c^{2}}{16}\left\lceil\frac{1+V_{T}}{Q}\right\rceil=\Omega(1+V_{T}).

∎

B.4
Proof of Theorem
3.6

This section is divided into two parts. The first part gives the proof of the upper bound of our fully single-loop algorithm
2
in Theorem
3.6
, and the second part briefly proves the possible improved bound of SOGD
[
30
]
in a deterministic environment.

Theorem B.12

(Restatement of Theorem
3.6
)
.

Under Assumptions
2.1
-
2.3
, let
\beta\leq\frac{1}{L_{g,1}}
,
\alpha\leq\frac{2\kappa_{F}\mu_{g}^{2}}{(\mu_{g}+L_{g,1})(\kappa_{F}\mu_{g}^{2}+C_{\beta})}
,
\gamma<\min\left\{\frac{1}{2L_{F}},\sqrt{\frac{(1-\rho)C_{1}}{12C_{\mathbf{x}}}}\right\}
,
N=1
and
M_{t}\equiv M=1
, Algorithm
2
can guarantee

\displaystyle\mathrm{Reg}(T)\leq

\displaystyle\frac{16Q}{\gamma C_{2}}+\frac{8}{\gamma C_{2}}V_{T}+\frac{6\Delta_{1}^{\prime}}{C_{1}C_{2}}+\frac{6C_{\alpha}}{(1-\rho)C_{1}C_{2}}H_{2,T}+\frac{6C_{P}}{(1-\rho)C_{1}C_{2}}E_{\mathbf{y},T}^{f}+\frac{6L_{f,0}^{2}C_{P}}{\mu_{g}^{2}(1-\rho)C_{1}C_{2}}E_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y},T}^{g}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(1+V_{T}+H_{2,T}+E_{2,T}),

where
\Delta_{1}^{\prime}
,
C_{1}
,
C_{2}
,
C_{\alpha}
,
C_{\beta}
,
C_{\mathbf{x}}
,
C_{P}
are some constants,
E_{2,T}=E_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y},T}^{g}+E_{\mathbf{y},T}^{f}
and

\displaystyle E_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y},T}^{g}:=\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sup_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t-1}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right\|^{2},\quad E_{\mathbf{y},T}^{f}:=\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sup_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t-1}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right\|^{2}.

Proof.

It begins with (
44
),

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2L_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{4L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,2}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t+1}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}+4L_{g,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t+1}-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(i)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(2L_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{4L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,2}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}+\frac{16L_{f,1}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}+\frac{16L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{g,2}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{4}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t+1}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}+8L_{g,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t+1}-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle\kappa_{F}\mu_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t+1}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}+8L_{g,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t+1}-\mathbf{w}_{t}^{*}\right\|^{2}=:\Delta_{t},

(54)

where
(i)
comes from the same fact in (
32
),
\kappa_{F}
reminds the same as (
47
) and
\mathbf{w}_{t}^{*}=\mathbf{v}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})
just for simplicity. Then substituting Lemma
B.5
and
B.6
into (
54
), with
\rho_{\mathbf{y}}:=1-\frac{\alpha\mu_{g}L_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+L_{g,1}}
and
\rho_{\mathbf{v}}:=1-\frac{\beta\mu_{g}}{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\Delta_{t}\leq

\displaystyle\kappa_{F}\mu_{g}^{2}\rho_{\mathbf{y}}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}-\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})\right\|^{2}+8L_{g,1}^{2}\rho_{\mathbf{v}}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t}-\mathbf{w}_{t-1}^{*}\right\|^{2}-\kappa_{F}\mu_{g}^{2}\left(\frac{2\alpha}{\mu_{g}+L_{g,1}}-\alpha^{2}\right)\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\kappa_{F}\mu_{g}^{2}\left(1+\frac{\mu_{g}+L_{g,1}}{\alpha\mu_{g}L_{g,1}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})\right\|^{2}+2\kappa_{g}^{2}\kappa_{F}\mu_{g}^{2}\left(1+\frac{\mu_{g}+L_{g,1}}{\alpha\mu_{g}L_{g,1}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}_{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+32\kappa_{g}^{2}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta\mu_{g}}\right)\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+32\kappa_{g}^{2}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta\mu_{g}}\right)\frac{L_{f,0}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+8L_{g,1}^{2}\left(\frac{4L_{f,1}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}+\frac{4L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,2}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{4}}\right)\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta\mu_{g}}\right)\left(\|\mathbf{x}_{t-1}-\mathbf{x}_{t}\|^{2}+\|\mathbf{y}_{t}-\mathbf{y}_{t+1}\|^{2}\right),

for simplicity, let

\displaystyle C_{\beta}:=8L_{g,1}^{2}\left(\frac{4L_{f,1}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}+\frac{4L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,2}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{4}}\right)\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta\mu_{g}}\right),\quad C_{\mathbf{x}}:=2\kappa_{g}^{2}\kappa_{F}\mu_{g}^{2}\left(1+\frac{\mu_{g}+L_{g,1}}{\alpha\mu_{g}L_{g,1}}\right)+C_{\beta},

\displaystyle C_{\alpha}:=2\kappa_{F}\mu_{g}^{2}\left(1+\frac{\mu_{g}+L_{g,1}}{\alpha\mu_{g}L_{g,1}}\right),\quad C_{P}:=32\kappa_{g}^{2}\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta\mu_{g}}\right),

then it was shown as

\displaystyle\Delta_{t}\leq

\displaystyle\kappa_{F}\mu_{g}^{2}\rho_{\mathbf{y}}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}{-}\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})\right\|^{2}+8L_{g,1}^{2}\rho_{\mathbf{v}}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t}{-}\mathbf{w}_{t-1}^{*}\right\|^{2}-\left(\frac{2\kappa_{F}\mu_{g}^{2}\alpha}{\mu_{g}+L_{g,1}}-\left(\kappa_{F}\mu_{g}^{2}{+}C_{\beta}\right)\alpha^{2}\right)\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})\right\|^{2}+C_{\mathbf{x}}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}_{t-1}\right\|^{2}+C_{P}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{L_{f,0}^{2}C_{P}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})\right\|^{2}.

(55)

Let
\alpha\leq\frac{2\kappa_{F}\mu_{g}^{2}}{(\mu_{g}+L_{g,1})(\kappa_{F}\mu_{g}^{2}+C_{\beta})}
, then we obtain

\displaystyle\Delta_{t}\leq

\displaystyle\kappa_{F}\mu_{g}^{2}\rho_{\mathbf{y}}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}{-}\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})\right\|^{2}+8L_{g,1}^{2}\rho_{\mathbf{v}}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t}{-}\mathbf{w}_{t-1}^{*}\right\|^{2}+C_{\mathbf{x}}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}{-}\mathbf{x}_{t-1}\right\|^{2}+C_{\alpha}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1}){-}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{P}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{L_{f,0}^{2}C_{P}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\rho\Delta_{t-1}+C_{\mathbf{x}}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}_{t-1}\right\|^{2}+C_{\alpha}\sup_{\mathbf{x}}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{*}(\mathbf{x})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x})\right\|^{2}+C_{P}\sup_{\mathbf{z}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t-1}(\mathbf{z})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t}(\mathbf{z})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{L_{f,0}^{2}C_{P}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\sup_{\mathbf{z}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t-1}(\mathbf{z})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{z})\right\|^{2},

where in the last inequality we set
\rho:=\max\{\rho_{\mathbf{y}},\rho_{\mathbf{v}}\}
. Telescoping the above inequality
t-1
times, we obtain

\displaystyle\Delta_{t}\leq

\displaystyle\rho^{t-1}\Delta_{1}+C_{\mathbf{x}}\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\rho^{j}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t-j}-\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j}\right\|^{2}+C_{\alpha}\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\rho^{j}\sup_{\mathbf{x}}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1-j}^{*}(\mathbf{x})-\mathbf{y}_{t-j}^{*}(\mathbf{x})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{P}\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\rho^{j}\sup_{\mathbf{z}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t-1-j}(\mathbf{z})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t-j}(\mathbf{z})\right\|^{2}+\frac{L_{f,0}^{2}C_{P}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\rho^{j}\sup_{\mathbf{z}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t-1-j}(\mathbf{z})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t-j}(\mathbf{z})\right\|^{2},

(56)

and substituting (
56
) into (
54
) and summing over
t=2,3,\dots,T
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T}\rho^{t-1}\Delta_{1}+C_{\mathbf{x}}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\rho^{j}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t-j}-\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j}\right\|^{2}+C_{\alpha}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\rho^{j}\sup_{\mathbf{x}}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1-j}^{*}(\mathbf{x})-\mathbf{y}_{t-j}^{*}(\mathbf{x})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{P}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\rho^{j}\sup_{\mathbf{z}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t-1-j}(\mathbf{z})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t-j}(\mathbf{z})\right\|^{2}+\frac{L_{f,0}^{2}C_{P}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\rho^{j}\sup_{\mathbf{z}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t-1-j}(\mathbf{z})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t-j}(\mathbf{z})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\Delta_{1}}{1-\rho}+\frac{C_{\mathbf{x}}}{1-\rho}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}_{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{C_{\alpha}}{1-\rho}H_{2,T}+\frac{C_{P}}{1-\rho}E_{\mathbf{y},T}^{f}+\frac{L_{f,0}^{2}C_{P}}{\mu_{g}^{2}(1-\rho)}E_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y},T}^{g},

where the last inequality comes from the fact that
\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\rho^{j}<\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho^{j}=\frac{1}{1-\rho}
for
\forall\rho\in(0,1)
. Then with (
63
),

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\Delta_{1}}{1-\rho}+\frac{\gamma^{2}C_{\mathbf{x}}}{1-\rho}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1}))\right\|^{2}+\frac{C_{\alpha}}{1-\rho}H_{2,T}+\frac{C_{P}}{1-\rho}E_{\mathbf{y},T}^{f}+\frac{L_{f,0}^{2}C_{P}}{\mu_{g}^{2}(1-\rho)}E_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y},T}^{g}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{2\gamma^{2}C_{\mathbf{x}}}{1-\rho}\right)\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla f_{1}(\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{y}_{1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{1}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{1}(\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{y}_{2},\mathbf{v}_{2})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Delta_{1}^{\prime}+\frac{2\gamma^{2}C_{\mathbf{x}}}{1-\rho}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})))\right\|^{2}+\frac{C_{\alpha}}{1-\rho}H_{2,T}+\frac{C_{P}}{1-\rho}E_{\mathbf{y},T}^{f}+\frac{L_{f,0}^{2}C_{P}}{\mu_{g}^{2}(1-\rho)}E_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y},T}^{g},

let
\gamma\leq\sqrt{\frac{1-\rho}{2C_{\mathbf{x}}}}
, the constant terms are integrated into
\Delta_{1}^{\prime}:=\frac{\Delta_{1}}{1-\rho}+\left\|\nabla f_{1}(\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{y}_{1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{1}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{1}(\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{y}_{2},\mathbf{v}_{2})\right\|^{2}
and
C_{1}:=1-\frac{2\gamma^{2}C_{\mathbf{x}}}{1-\rho}
, it implies that

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\Delta_{1}^{\prime}}{C_{1}}+\frac{2\gamma^{2}C_{\mathbf{x}}}{(1-\rho)C_{1}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{\alpha}}{(1-\rho)C_{1}}H_{2,T}+\frac{C_{P}}{(1-\rho)C_{1}}E_{\mathbf{y},T}^{f}+\frac{L_{f,0}^{2}C_{P}}{\mu_{g}^{2}(1-\rho)C_{1}}E_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y},T}^{g}.

(57)

Now, remind of Lemma
B.8
, we still have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma\right)\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\gamma\theta}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t+1},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t+1}))\right)

\displaystyle+\left(2+\frac{1}{\lambda\theta}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})\right\|^{2},

(58)

after substituting (
57
) into (
58
), we finally obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\gamma\theta}(2Q+V_{T})+\left(2+\frac{1}{\lambda\theta}\right)\left(\frac{\Delta_{1}^{\prime}}{C_{1}}+\frac{C_{\alpha}}{(1-\rho)C_{1}}H_{2,T}+\frac{C_{P}}{(1-\rho)C_{1}}E_{\mathbf{y},T}^{f}+\frac{L_{f,0}^{2}C_{P}}{\mu_{g}^{2}(1-\rho)C_{1}}E_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y},T}^{g}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(2+\frac{1}{\lambda\theta}\right)\frac{2\gamma^{2}C_{\mathbf{x}}}{(1-\rho)C_{1}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})))\right\|^{2}

let
\gamma\leq\frac{1}{2L_{F}}
and
\lambda=1
, then
\theta=1-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\gamma L_{F}}{2}\geq\frac{1}{4}
, which implies that

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{12\gamma^{2}C_{\mathbf{x}}}{(1-\rho)C_{1}}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{16Q}{\gamma}+\frac{8}{\gamma}V_{T}+\frac{6\Delta_{1}^{\prime}}{C_{1}}+\frac{6C_{\alpha}}{(1-\rho)C_{1}}H_{2,T}+\frac{6C_{P}}{(1-\rho)C_{1}}E_{\mathbf{y},T}^{f}+\frac{6L_{f,0}^{2}C_{P}}{\mu_{g}^{2}(1-\rho)C_{1}}E_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y},T}^{g},

To ensure
C_{2}:=1-\frac{12\gamma^{2}C_{\mathbf{x}}}{(1-\rho)C_{1}}>0
we set

\displaystyle\gamma\leq\sqrt{\frac{(1-\rho)C_{1}}{12C_{\mathbf{x}}}}<\sqrt{\frac{1-\rho}{2C_{\mathbf{x}}}}.

Finally, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{16Q}{\gamma C_{2}}+\frac{8}{\gamma C_{2}}V_{T}+\frac{6\Delta_{1}^{\prime}}{C_{1}C_{2}}+\frac{6C_{\alpha}}{(1-\rho)C_{1}C_{2}}H_{2,T}+\frac{6C_{P}}{(1-\rho)C_{1}C_{2}}E_{\mathbf{y},T}^{f}+\frac{6L_{f,0}^{2}C_{P}}{\mu_{g}^{2}(1-\rho)C_{1}C_{2}}E_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y},T}^{g}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(1+V_{T}+H_{2,T}+E_{2,T}),

where
E_{2,T}:=E_{\mathbf{y},T}^{f}+E_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y},T}^{g}
to finish the proof.
∎

B.5
Proof of SOBOW under Standard Bilevel Local Regret

In this section, we prove that SOBOW
[
25
]
can obtain an upper bound
O(1+V_{T}+H_{2,T})
under regret defined in (
2
) with
w=1
.

Lemma B.13

(
[
25
]
)
.

Under Assumptions
2.1
,
2.2
, consider the inner iteration process of
g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\cdot)
in Algorithm
4
, set
\alpha<\frac{1}{L_{g,1}}
we can have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{y}_{t+1}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\alpha\mu_{g}}{2}\right)\|\mathbf{y}_{t}-\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})\|^{2}+2\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\right)(1-\alpha\mu_{g})\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\right)\frac{(1-\alpha\mu_{g})L_{g,1}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\|\mathbf{x}_{t-1}-\mathbf{x}_{t}\|^{2}.

Lemma B.14

(Based on Lemma 5.5 in
[
25
]
)
.

Under Assumptions
2.1
,
2.2
, let
\alpha\leq\frac{1}{L_{g,1}}
,
\lambda\leq\frac{1}{L_{g,1}}
and
M_{t+1}-M_{t}\geq\frac{\log(1-\frac{\alpha\mu_{g}}{2})}{2\log(1-\lambda\mu_{g})}
. We can have that

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t+1}-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))\right\|^{2}\leq c\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t+1}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}+\epsilon_{t}^{2},

where
c>0
is some constant and the error
\epsilon_{t}^{2}
decays with
t
, i.e.,
\epsilon_{t+1}^{2}\leq\left(1-\frac{\alpha\mu_{g}}{2}\right)\epsilon_{t}^{2}
.

Remark B.15
.

[
25
]
Lemma 5.5 above was obtained under weaker assumptions. They assumed that there exists at least one point
\mathbf{x}^{\prime}
in
\mathcal{X}
such that the gradient
\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime}))\right\|
has an upper bound
\rho
. We do not have this assumption, but note that the upper bound of the gradient still has
L_{f,0}
, due to the lipschitz continuity in Assumption
2.2
, the above lemma still holds under our stronger assumptions.

Algorithm 4

SOBOW
[
25
]
without window averaging

Input
: Initializations:
\mathbf{x}_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}},\mathbf{y}_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{2}}
,
\mathbf{v}_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{2}}
.

Parameters
: step size
\alpha>0
,
\beta>0
,
\gamma>0
.

Output
: Decision sequences:
\{\mathbf{x}_{t}\}_{t=1}^{T}
,
\{\mathbf{y}_{t}\}_{t=1}^{T}
.

1:

for

t=1
to
T

do

2:

Output
\mathbf{x}_{t}
and
\mathbf{y}_{t}
, receive
f_{t}
and
g_{t}

3:

\mathbf{y}_{t+1}\leftarrow\mathbf{y}_{t}-\alpha\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})

4:

Solve
\mathop{\arg\min}_{\mathbf{v}\in\mathcal{V}}\Phi_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v})
defined in (
7
) using
M_{t}
steps of conjugate gradient starting from a fixed
\mathbf{v}^{0}
with step size
\lambda
to obtain
\mathbf{v}_{t+1}

5:

Calculate
\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})
by (
8
) and use it to update
\mathbf{x}_{t+1}
by (
3
)

6:

end

for

Theorem B.16
.

Under Assumptions
2.1
-
2.3
, let
\alpha\leq\frac{1}{L_{g,1}}
,
\lambda\leq\frac{1}{L_{g,1}}
,
M_{t+1}-M_{t}\geq\frac{\log(1-\frac{\alpha\mu_{g}}{2})}{2\log(1-\lambda\mu_{g})}
and
\gamma<\min\left\{\frac{1}{2L_{F}},\frac{\alpha\mu_{g}}{4\kappa_{g}}\sqrt{\frac{C_{1}}{6C_{\mathbf{y}}}}\right\}
where
L_{F}
is the smoothness parameter of
f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))
with respect to
\mathbf{x}
, Algorithm
4
can guarantee

\displaystyle\mathrm{Reg}(T)\leq

\displaystyle\frac{16Q}{\gamma C_{2}}+\frac{8}{\gamma C_{2}}V_{T}+\frac{6}{C_{2}}\left\|\nabla f_{1}(\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{y}_{1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{1})){-}\widetilde{\nabla}f_{1}(\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{y}_{2},\mathbf{v}_{2})\right\|^{2}+\frac{6\Delta_{1}}{(1{-}\rho)C_{1}C_{2}}+\frac{24C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}(1{-}\rho)C_{1}C_{2}}H_{2,T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(1+V_{T}+H_{2,T}),

where
\rho=1-\frac{\alpha\mu_{g}}{2}
,
C_{1}
,
C_{2}
and
C_{\mathbf{y}}
are some constants.

Proof.

It begins with (
44
),

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2L_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{4L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,2}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t+1}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}+4L_{g,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t+1}-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2},

(59)

with substituting Lemma
B.14
into (
59
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})\right\|^{2}\leq\left(2L_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{4L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,2}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}+c\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t+1}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}+4L_{g,1}^{2}\epsilon_{t}^{2}=:\Delta_{t},

for simplicity, define
C_{\mathbf{y}}:=2L_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{4L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,2}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}+c
. With Lemma
B.13
, it holds that

\displaystyle\Delta_{t}\leq

\displaystyle C_{\mathbf{y}}\rho\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}-\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})\right\|^{2}+4\rho L_{g,1}^{2}\epsilon_{t-1}^{2}+2C_{\mathbf{y}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\right)(1-\alpha\mu_{g})\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\kappa_{g}^{2}C_{\mathbf{y}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\right)(1-\alpha\mu_{g})\|\mathbf{x}_{t-1}-\mathbf{x}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\rho\Delta_{t-1}+\frac{4C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{4\kappa_{g}^{2}C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\|\mathbf{x}_{t-1}-\mathbf{x}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\rho^{t-1}\Delta_{1}+\frac{4C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\rho^{j}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1-j}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j})-\mathbf{y}_{t-j}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j})\right\|^{2}+\frac{4\kappa_{g}^{2}C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\rho^{j}\|\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j}-\mathbf{x}_{t-j}\|^{2}.

(60)

Finally, substituting (
60
) into (
59
) we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\rho^{t-1}\Delta_{1}+\frac{4C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\rho^{j}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1-j}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j})-\mathbf{y}_{t-j}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j})\right\|^{2}+\frac{4\kappa_{g}^{2}C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\rho^{j}\|\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j}-\mathbf{x}_{t-j}\|^{2}.

(61)

Then, summing (
61
) over
t=2,\dots,T
to obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T}\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T}\rho^{t-1}\Delta_{1}+\frac{4C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\rho^{j}\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1-j}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j})-\mathbf{y}_{t-j}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j})\|^{2}+\frac{4\kappa_{g}^{2}C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\rho^{j}\|\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j}-\mathbf{x}_{t-j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(i)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{\Delta_{1}}{1-\rho}+\frac{4C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}(1-\rho)}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})\|^{2}+\frac{4\kappa_{g}^{2}C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}(1-\rho)}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\|\mathbf{x}_{t-1}-\mathbf{x}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(ii)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{\Delta_{1}}{1-\rho}+\frac{4C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}(1-\rho)}H_{2,T}+\frac{4\kappa_{g}^{2}C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}(1-\rho)}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t-1}-\mathbf{x}_{t}\right\|^{2},

(62)

where
(i)
comes from the fact that
\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\rho^{j}<\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\rho^{j}=\frac{1}{1-\rho}
for
\forall\rho\in(0,1)
, and
(ii)
follows the definition of
H_{2,T}
in (
5
). Then note that

\displaystyle\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}_{t-1}=\gamma\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1},\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1},\mathbf{y}_{t},\mathbf{v}_{t}),\gamma),

(63)

and substitute it into (
62
) we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T}\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\Delta_{1}}{1-\rho}+\frac{4C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}(1-\rho)}H_{2,T}+\frac{4\kappa_{g}^{2}C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}(1-\rho)}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1}),\gamma)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{8\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}(1-\rho)}\right)\sum_{t=2}^{T}\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\Delta_{1}}{1-\rho}+\frac{4C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}(1-\rho)}H_{2,T}+\frac{8\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}(1-\rho)}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma)\right\|^{2},

let
C_{1}:=1-\frac{8\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}(1-\rho)}>0
, set
\gamma<\frac{\alpha\mu_{g}}{4\kappa_{g}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{C_{\mathbf{y}}}}
, it then holds that

\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T}\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\Delta_{1}}{(1-\rho)C_{1}}+\frac{4C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}(1-\rho)C_{1}}H_{2,T}+\frac{8\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}(1-\rho)C_{1}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma)\right\|^{2}.

(64)

Now, remind of Lemma
B.8
,

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\gamma\theta}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t+1},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t+1}))\right)+\left(2+\frac{1}{\lambda\theta}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})\right\|^{2},

(65)

and substituting (
64
) into it, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4Q}{\gamma\theta}+\frac{2}{\gamma\theta}V_{T}+\left(2+\frac{1}{\lambda\theta}\right)\left\|\nabla f_{1}(\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{y}_{1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{1}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{1}(\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{y}_{2},\mathbf{v}_{2})\right\|^{2}+\left(2+\frac{1}{\lambda\theta}\right)\frac{\Delta_{1}}{(1-\rho)C_{1}}

\displaystyle+\left(2+\frac{1}{\lambda\theta}\right)\frac{4C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}(1-\rho)C_{1}}H_{2,T}+\left(2+\frac{1}{\lambda\theta}\right)\frac{8\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}(1-\rho)C_{1}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma)\right\|^{2},

(66)

let
\lambda=1
and
\gamma\leq\frac{1}{2L_{F}}
, thus
\theta=1-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\gamma L_{F}}{2}\geq\frac{1}{4}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{48\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}(1-\rho)C_{1}}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{16Q}{\gamma}+\frac{8}{\gamma}V_{T}+6\left\|\nabla f_{1}(\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{y}_{1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{1}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{1}(\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{y}_{2},\mathbf{v}_{2})\right\|^{2}+\frac{6\Delta_{1}}{(1-\rho)C_{1}}+\frac{24C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}(1-\rho)C_{1}}H_{2,T},

with
\gamma\leq\frac{\alpha\mu_{g}}{4\kappa_{g}}\sqrt{\frac{C_{1}}{6C_{\mathbf{y}}}}
and
C_{2}:=1-\frac{48\gamma^{2}\kappa_{F}L_{g,1}^{2}}{(1-\rho)C_{1}}
, which implies that

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{16}{\gamma C_{2}}+\frac{8}{\gamma C_{2}}V_{T}+\frac{6}{C_{2}}\left\|\nabla f_{1}(\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{y}_{1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{1}))-\widetilde{\nabla}f_{1}(\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{y}_{2},\mathbf{v}_{2})\right\|^{2}+\frac{6\Delta_{1}}{(1-\rho)C_{1}C_{2}}+\frac{24C_{\mathbf{y}}}{\alpha\mu_{g}(1-\rho)C_{1}C_{2}}H_{2,T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(1+V_{T}+H_{2,T}).

Thus we finish the proof.
∎

B.6
Proof of OBBO under Standard Bilevel Local Regret

Algorithm 5

OBBO
[
4
]
without window averaging

Input
: Initializations:
\mathbf{x}_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}},\mathbf{y}_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{2}}
.

Parameters
: step size
\eta>0
,
\alpha>0
.

Output
: Decision sequences:
\{\mathbf{x}_{t}\}_{t=1}^{T}
,
\{\mathbf{y}_{t}\}_{t=1}^{T}
.

1:

for

t=1
to
T

do

2:

Output
\mathbf{x}_{t}
and
\mathbf{y}_{t}
, receive
f_{t}
and
g_{t}

3:

\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\leftarrow\mathbf{y}_{t}

4:

for

k=1
to
K

do

5:

\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k}\leftarrow\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k-1}-\alpha\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})

6:

end

for

7:

Calculate
\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{K}):=\frac{\partial f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{K})}{\partial\mathbf{x}}
by (
67
) and set
\mathbf{y}_{t+1}=\mathbf{y}_{t}^{K}

8:

use
\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1})
to update
\mathbf{x}_{t+1}
by (
3
)

9:

end

for

In this section, we prove that OBBO
[
4
]
can obtain an upper bound
O(1+V_{T}+H_{2,T})
under regret defined in (
2
) with
w=1
. The following lemma shows the ITD approach approximating the hypergradient used by Algorithm
5
.

Lemma B.17

(Lemma 4.2 in
[
4
]
)
.

The partial
\frac{\partial f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{K})}{\partial\mathbf{x}}
takes an analytical form of

\displaystyle\frac{\partial f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{K})}{\partial\mathbf{x}}=\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{K})-\eta\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{K}),

(67)

where
\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}:=\prod_{j=k+1}^{K-1}\left(\mathbf{I}_{d_{2}}-\eta\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{j})\right)
.

The following lemma gives the hypergradient estimation error upper bound at time step
t
in OBBO.

Lemma B.18

(Lemma 5.1 in
[
4
]
)
.

Under Assumptions
2.1
-
2.3
, let
\eta\leq\min\{\frac{1}{\mu_{g}},\frac{1}{L_{g,1}}\}
and
K\geq 1
, we then obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{K})}{\partial\mathbf{x}}-\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta_{t}+A\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\nu^{j}\left\|\frac{\partial f_{t-1-j,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j},\mathbf{y}_{t-1-j}^{K})}{\partial\mathbf{x}}-\nabla F_{t-1-j,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+B\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\nu^{j}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j},\nabla F_{t-1-j,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j}),\alpha)\right\|^{2}+C\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\nu^{j}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-j}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j})-\mathbf{y}_{t-1-j}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j})\right\|^{2},

where
\delta_{t}=3L_{3}^{2}(1-\eta\mu_{g})^{2K}+3L_{\beta}\nu^{t-1}\Delta_{\beta}
,
\nu=(1-\frac{\eta\mu_{g}}{2})(1-\eta\mu_{g})^{K-1}
with
L_{3}
,
L_{\beta}
,
\Delta_{\beta}
,
A
,
B
and
C
are some constants.

Theorem B.19
.

Under Assumptions
2.1
-
2.3
, let
\eta\leq\min\{\frac{1}{\mu_{g}},\frac{1}{L_{g,1}}\}
,
\alpha\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{2L_{F}},\frac{\rho\sqrt{1-\nu}}{\kappa_{g}\sqrt{108C_{\mu_{g}}L_{\beta}}},\frac{\sqrt{1-\nu}}{9\kappa_{g}\sqrt{C_{\mu_{g}}L_{\beta}}}\right\}
and
K=\frac{\log T}{\log((1-\eta\mu_{g})^{-1})}+1
where
L_{F}
is the smoothness parameter of
f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))
with respect to
\mathbf{x}
, Algorithm
5
can guarantee

\displaystyle\mathrm{Reg}(T)\leq

\displaystyle\frac{16Q+8V_{T}}{c\gamma}+\frac{51}{4c}\left(\frac{\Delta_{\beta}L_{\beta}}{1-\nu}+L_{3}^{2}\right)+\frac{27CH_{2,T}}{4c(1-\nu)}+\frac{6}{c}\left\|\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})-\frac{\partial f_{1}(\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{y}_{1}^{K})}{\partial\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(1+V_{T}+H_{2,T}),

where
c
,
C
,
\Delta_{\beta}
,
L_{\beta}
are some constants,
\nu=(1-\frac{\eta\mu_{g}}{2})(1-\eta\mu_{g})
.

Proof.

It begins with Lemma
B.18
with
w=1
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{K})}{\partial\mathbf{x}}-\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta_{t}+A\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\nu^{j}\left\|\frac{\partial f_{t-1-j}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j},\mathbf{y}_{t-1-j}^{K})}{\partial\mathbf{x}}-\nabla F_{t-1-j}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+B\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\nu^{j}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j},\nabla F_{t-1-j}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j}),\alpha)\right\|^{2}+C\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\nu^{j}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-j}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j})-\mathbf{y}_{t-1-j}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j})\right\|^{2},

summing the above inequality, we then obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left\|\frac{\partial f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{K})}{\partial\mathbf{x}}-\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3L_{3}^{2}T(1-\eta\mu_{g})^{2K}+3L_{\beta}\Delta_{\beta}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\nu^{t-1}+A\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\nu^{j}\left\|\frac{\partial f_{t-1-j}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j},\mathbf{y}_{t-1-j}^{K})}{\partial\mathbf{x}}-\nabla F_{t-1-j}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+B\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\nu^{j}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j},\nabla F_{t-1-j}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j}),\alpha)\right\|^{2}+C\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\nu^{j}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-j}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j})-\mathbf{y}_{t-1-j}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1-j})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3L_{3}^{2}+\frac{3L_{\beta}\Delta_{\beta}}{1-\nu}+\frac{A}{1-\nu}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left\|\frac{\partial f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{K})}{\partial\mathbf{x}}-\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{B}{1-\nu}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}),\alpha)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{1-\nu}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})-\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1})\right\|^{2},

where the last inequality comes from the fact that
\sum_{j=0}^{t-2}\nu^{j}<\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\nu^{j}=\frac{1}{1-\nu}
for
\forall\rho\in(0,1)
. After rearranging the terms, it holds that

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{A}{1-\nu}\right)\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left\|\frac{\partial f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{K})}{\partial\mathbf{x}}-\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle 3L_{3}^{2}+\frac{3L_{\beta}\Delta_{\beta}}{1-\nu}+\frac{B}{1-\nu}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}),\alpha)\right\|^{2}+\frac{CH_{2,T}}{1-\nu},

(68)

with the expression of
A
in Lemma B.3
[
4
]
,

\displaystyle A=\frac{12\alpha^{2}C_{\mu_{g}}L_{\beta}\kappa_{g}^{2}}{\rho^{2}},\quad 0<\alpha\leq\frac{\rho\sqrt{1-\nu}}{\kappa_{g}\sqrt{108C_{\mu_{g}}L_{\beta}}},

substituting the above inequality into (
68
), it follows that

\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left\|\frac{\partial f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{K})}{\partial\mathbf{x}}-\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{27}{8}\left(\frac{\Delta_{\beta}L_{\beta}}{1-\nu}+L_{3}^{2}\right)+\frac{9B}{8(1-\nu)}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}),\alpha)\right\|^{2}+\frac{9CH_{2,T}}{8(1-\nu)}.

(69)

Now remind of Lemma
B.8
, with
\widetilde{\nabla}f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t+1},\mathbf{v}_{t+1})=\frac{\partial f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{K})}{\partial\mathbf{x}}
, we naturally have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\gamma\theta}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t+1},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t+1}))\right)+\left(2+\frac{1}{\lambda\theta}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\frac{\partial f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{K})}{\partial\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}.

(70)

we still set
\alpha\leq\frac{1}{2L_{F}}
and
\lambda=1
,
2+\frac{1}{\lambda\theta}\leq 6
, the same as in Theorem
B.16
, also with Lemma
B.7
, it then holds that

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}),\gamma\right)\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4Q+2V_{T}}{\gamma\theta}+\frac{51}{4}\left(\frac{\Delta_{\beta}L_{\beta}}{1-\nu}+L_{3}^{2}\right)+\frac{27B}{4(1-\nu)}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}),\alpha)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{27CH_{2,T}}{4(1-\nu)}+6\left\|\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})-\frac{\partial f_{1}(\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{y}_{1}^{K})}{\partial\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2},

with
B=12\alpha^{2}C_{\mu_{g}}L_{\beta}\kappa_{g}^{2}
in Lemma B.3
[
4
]
,

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{81\alpha^{2}C_{\mu_{g}}L_{\beta}\kappa_{g}^{2}}{1-\nu}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}),\gamma\right)\right\|^{2}

(71)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4Q+2V_{T}}{\gamma\theta}+\frac{51}{4}\left(\frac{\Delta_{\beta}L_{\beta}}{1-\nu}+L_{3}^{2}\right)+\frac{27CH_{2,T}}{4(1-\nu)}+6\left\|\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})-\frac{\partial f_{1}(\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{y}_{1}^{K})}{\partial\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}.

(72)

We set

\displaystyle\alpha\leq\frac{\sqrt{1-\nu}}{9\kappa_{g}\sqrt{C_{\mu_{g}}L_{\beta}}}\quad\text{and}\quad c:=1-\frac{81\alpha^{2}C_{\mu_{g}}L_{\beta}\kappa_{g}^{2}}{1-\nu},

and substitute into (
72
) to get

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t}),\gamma\right)\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4Q+2V_{T}}{c\gamma\theta}+\frac{51}{4c}\left(\frac{\Delta_{\beta}L_{\beta}}{1-\nu}+L_{3}^{2}\right)+\frac{27CH_{2,T}}{4c(1-\nu)}+\frac{6}{c}\left\|\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})-\frac{\partial f_{1}(\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{y}_{1}^{K})}{\partial\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(1+V_{T}+H_{2,T}).

Thus we finish the proof.
∎

B.7
Proof of SOGD in Deterministic Online Bilevel Optimization

In the work of
[
30
]
, the objective functions
f_{t}(\cdot)
,
g_{t}(\cdot)
in problem (
1
) they studied had the following random noise:

\displaystyle f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))=\mathbb{E}_{\xi_{t}\sim\mathcal{D}_{f}}\left[f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x});\xi_{t})\right],\quad g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=\mathbb{E}_{\zeta_{t}\sim\mathcal{D}_{g}}\left[g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y};\zeta_{t})\right],

where
\mathcal{D}_{f},\mathcal{D}_{g}
are data distributions. The unbiased stochastic gradient is revealed, and the variance follows the bounded assumption as follows:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\|\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y};\xi_{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\|^{2}\right]\leq\sigma_{f\mathbf{x}}^{2},\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y};\zeta)-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\|^{2}\right]\leq\sigma_{g\mathbf{y}}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\|\nabla^{2}_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y};\zeta)-\nabla^{2}_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\|^{2}\right]\leq\sigma_{g\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2},\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\|\nabla^{2}_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y};\zeta)-\nabla^{2}_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\|^{2}\right]\leq\sigma_{g\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y};\xi_{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\|^{2}\right]\leq\sigma_{f\mathbf{y}}^{2},

they also define
\sigma^{2}:=\sigma_{g\mathbf{y}}^{2}+\sigma_{f\mathbf{y}}^{2}+\sigma_{g\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}+\sigma_{g\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}+\sigma_{f\mathbf{x}}^{2}
.

We now briefly demonstrate the improved bound of SOGD in a deterministic environment, i.e.,
\sigma=0
.

Theorem B.20
.

Under Assumptions
2.1
-
2.3
, let
\alpha_{t}\equiv\alpha
,
\beta_{t}\equiv\beta=c_{\beta}\alpha
,
\delta_{t}\equiv\delta=c_{\delta}\alpha
,
\gamma_{t}\equiv\gamma=c_{\gamma}\alpha^{2}
,
\eta_{t}\equiv\eta=c_{\eta}\alpha^{2}
and
\lambda_{t}\equiv\lambda=c_{\lambda}\alpha^{2}
for some constants
c
,
c_{\beta}
,
c_{\delta}
,
c_{\gamma}
,
c_{\eta}
and
c_{\lambda}
, SOGD
[
30
]
can guarantees the following bound on the bilevel local regret defined in (
2
) for problem (
1
):

\displaystyle\mathrm{Reg}(T)\leq O\left(1+V_{T}+H_{2,T}+E_{2,T}+P_{T}\right),

where
P_{T}
is defined in (
75
).

Proof.

We begin with equation (122) in their proof of Section C.6, shown as:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\alpha_{t}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]{+}\Lambda{\leq}O\left(V_{T}{+}\frac{H_{2,T}}{\alpha_{T}}{+}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{b}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\alpha_{t}^{3}{+}\frac{G_{\mathbf{y},T}{+}G_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y},T}{+}G_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y},T}{+}D_{\mathbf{x},T}{+}D_{\mathbf{y},T}}{\alpha_{T}}\right),

where

\displaystyle D_{\mathbf{x},T}:=\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sup_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f_{t-1}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right\|^{2},\quad D_{\mathbf{y},T}:=\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sup_{\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t-1}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right\|^{2},

\displaystyle G_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y},T}:=\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})\right\|^{2},\quad G_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y},T}:=\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})\right\|^{2},

\displaystyle G_{\mathbf{y},T}:=\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})\right\|^{2},\quad\Psi_{T}:=G_{\mathbf{y},T}+G_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y},T}+G_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y},T}+D_{\mathbf{x},T}+D_{\mathbf{y},T}.

(73)

With
\Lambda\geq-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\alpha_{0}}
and
\alpha_{t}=\frac{1}{\sqrt[3]{c+t}}
for some constant
c
is set, which means
\sum_{t=1}^{T}\alpha_{t}^{3}\leq\log(T+1)
, the stochastic bilevel local regret bound is achieved as

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\alpha_{t}\right)\right\|^{2}\right]\leq

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{T}}(\sigma^{2}+V_{T})+\frac{1}{\alpha_{T}^{2}}\Psi_{T}\right)

(74)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(T^{1/3}(\sigma^{2}+V_{T})+T^{2/3}\Psi_{T}\right).

Now consider the deterministic situation, which means
\sigma=0
and (
74
) becomes

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\alpha_{t}\right)\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{T}}(1+V_{T})+\frac{1}{\alpha_{T}^{2}}(H_{2,T}+G_{\mathbf{y},T}+G_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y},T}+G_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y},T}+D_{\mathbf{x},T}+D_{\mathbf{y},T})\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{T}}(1+V_{T})+\frac{1}{\alpha_{T}^{2}}(H_{2,T}+E_{2,T}+P_{T}\right),

where
E_{2,T}
is defined in Theorem
3.6
and

\displaystyle P_{T}:=D_{\mathbf{x},T}+G_{\mathbf{y},T}+G_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y},T}.

(75)

Note that we can now set
\alpha_{t}\equiv\alpha=\frac{1}{c}
, since term
\sum_{t=1}^{T}\alpha_{t}^{3}
is already zero, and the step size no longer needs to decay with
t
to ensure this term is sublinear. We thus obtain the following upper-bound guarantee for SOGD in the noise-free setting:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Reg}(T)\leq O\left(1+V_{T}+H_{2,T}+E_{2,T}+P_{T}\right).

∎

Appendix C
Proofs of Section
4
under Window-Averaged Bilevel Local Regret

In this section, we provide proofs of Theorems
4.3
and
4.4
under the window averaged bilevel local regret presented in Section
4
. In Section
C.1
, we present all the lemmas used in this section, and in Sections
C.2
and
C.3
, we present the proofs of Theorem
4.3
and
4.4
, respectively.

C.1
Preliminary Lemmas

Note that
\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\cdot)
and
\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\cdot)
are linear combinations of
f_{t}(\cdot)
and
g_{t}(\cdot)
for some
t\in[T]
, and therefore also satisfy assumptions
2.1
-
2.3
. We similarily have

\displaystyle\nabla\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))=

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),

\displaystyle\text{where}\quad\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))=

\displaystyle\left[\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))\right]^{-1}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})))

and define

\displaystyle\widehat{\Phi}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{v}):=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{v}^{\top}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\mathbf{v}-\mathbf{v}^{\top}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2W}\sum_{i=0}^{w-1}\eta^{i}\mathbf{v}^{\top}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t-i}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})-\frac{1}{W}\sum_{i=0}^{w-1}\eta^{i}\mathbf{v}^{\top}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t-i}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}),

it also holds that

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{v}}\widehat{\Phi}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{v})=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{W}\sum_{i=0}^{w-1}\eta^{i}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t-i}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\mathbf{v}-\frac{1}{W}\sum_{i=0}^{w-1}\eta^{i}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{t-i}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=\frac{1}{W}\sum_{i=0}^{w-1}\eta^{i}\nabla_{\mathbf{v}}\Phi_{t-i}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{v}).

Due to the
\mu_{g}
-strongly convex and
L_{g,1}
-smooth of
\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\cdot)
for any
\mathbf{x}\in\mathcal{X}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{1})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{2})\right\|\leq L_{g,1}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{1}-\mathbf{y}_{2}\right\|

(76)

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{1})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{2})\right\|\geq\mu_{g}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{1}-\mathbf{y}_{2}\right\|.

(77)

Lemma C.1

(Proposition 2.4 in
[
15
]
)
.

Let
\mathbf{x}
be any point in
\mathcal{X}\subsetneq\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}}
, and let
f
,
g
be differentiable functions
\mathcal{X}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
. Then for any
\gamma>0
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x},\nabla f(\mathbf{x})+\nabla g(\mathbf{x}),\gamma)\right\|\leq\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x},\nabla f(\mathbf{x}),\gamma)\right\|+\left\|\nabla g(\mathbf{x})\right\|.

Lemma C.2
.

Under Assumptions
2.1
,
2.2
and
4.2
, for all
t\in[T]
and any
\mathbf{x}\in\mathcal{X}
we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x})-\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x})\right\|\leq\frac{(1+\eta^{w})L_{g,1}D}{\mu_{g}W}.

Proof.

It begins (
77
), with
\mathbf{y}_{2}=\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x})
, we have

\displaystyle\mu_{g}\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x})\right\|\leq\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))\right\|=\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right\|,

let
\mathbf{y}=\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x})
, it implies that

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x})-\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x})\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mu_{g}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mu_{g}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))-\eta\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))\right\|,

then it natually holds that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))-\eta\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{W}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))-\eta^{w}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t-w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{W}\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{w-1}\eta^{i}\left(\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t-i}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t-i}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))\right)\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{W}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))-\eta^{w}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t-w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))\right\|\leq\frac{(1+\eta^{w})L_{g,1}D}{W},

where in the last inequality, notice that for any
\mathbf{y}\in\mathcal{Y}
defined in Assumption
4.2
, with
\mathbf{y}_{2}=\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x})
in (
20
),

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right\|=\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))\right\|\leq L_{g,1}\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x})\right\|\leq L_{g,1}D.

Thus we finish the proof.
∎

Lemma C.3
.

Under Assumptions
2.1
,
2.2
and
4.2
, for all
t\in[T]
and any
\mathbf{x}\in\mathcal{X}
we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})-\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right\|\leq\frac{(1+\eta^{w})L_{f,0}L_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}^{2}W}.

Proof.

The proof is the same as Lemma
C.2
, and note that
\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\in\mathcal{V}
for any
\mathbf{x}\in\mathcal{X}
,
\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}_{d_{2}}
, thus

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})-\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mu_{g}W}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{v}}\Phi_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{v}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}))-\eta^{w}\nabla_{\mathbf{v}}\Phi_{t-w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{v}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}))\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{(1+\eta^{w})L_{g,1}L_{f,0}}{\mu_{g}^{2}W}.

∎

Lemma C.4
.

Under Assumptions
2.1
,
2.2
, for all
t\in[T]
, any
\mathbf{x}\in\mathcal{X}
,
\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{2}}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right\|\leq L_{\mathbf{v}}\quad\text{and}\quad\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right\|\leq L_{\mathbf{v}},

where
L_{\mathbf{v}}:=\frac{L_{f,1}}{\mu_{g}}+\frac{L_{f,0}L_{g,2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}
.

Proof.

Note that

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{v}}\widehat{\Phi}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}))=\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=0,

taking the derivative of the above equation with respect to
\mathbf{x}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}\mathbf{x}}^{3}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})+\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{x}}^{2}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}),

thus

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=

\displaystyle\left[\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right]^{-1}\left[\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{x}}^{2}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}\mathbf{x}}^{3}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right]

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mu_{g}}\left(L_{f,1}+\frac{L_{g,2}L_{f,0}}{\mu_{g}}\right)=\frac{L_{f,1}}{\mu_{g}}+\frac{L_{f,0}L_{g,2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}=:L_{\mathbf{v}},

where the last inequality comes from Assumption
2.1
and
2.2
. Similarily, we have
\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right\|\leq L_{\mathbf{v}}
.
∎

Lemma C.5
.

Under Assumptions
2.1
,
2.2
, for all
t\in[T]
, consider the sequences
\{\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}\}_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}
generated by Algorithm
3
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k}\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\right)\frac{2\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8-4\alpha\mu_{g}}{\alpha^{3}\mu_{g}}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\right)\frac{2\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}(4-2\alpha\mu_{g})}{\alpha^{3}\mu_{g}}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2}.

Proof.

Let
\alpha\leq\frac{1}{L_{g,1}}
, due to the strongly convexity of
\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\cdot)
we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k+1}-\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k})\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\alpha\mu_{g}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k}-\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k+1}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k}\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle 2\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k+1}-\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k})\right\|^{2}+2\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(4-2\alpha\mu_{g}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k}-\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k})\right\|^{2},

(78)

additionally, it follows that

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+\theta)\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k-1})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k}\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{1}{\theta}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k})-\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(i)}{\leq}

\displaystyle(1+\theta)(1-\alpha\mu_{g})\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k-1})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k-1}\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{1}{\theta}\right)\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k-1},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k-1},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k-1},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k-1}),\gamma)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(ii)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\alpha\mu_{g}}{2}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k-1})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k-1}\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\right)\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k-1},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k-1},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k-1},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k-1}),\gamma)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\rho_{\mathbf{y}}^{k}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\right)\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}\rho_{\mathbf{y}}^{k-1-j}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{j},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{j},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{j},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{j}),\gamma))\right\|^{2},

(79)

where
(i)
comes from the fact that
\left\|\nabla\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x})\right\|\leq\kappa_{g}
following Lemma
B.2
, and in
(ii)
set
\theta=\frac{\alpha\mu_{g}}{2}
, then
\left(1+\frac{\alpha\mu_{g}}{2}\right)(1-\alpha\mu_{g})\leq 1-\frac{\alpha\mu_{g}}{2}
,
\rho_{\mathbf{y}}:=1-\frac{\alpha\mu_{g}}{2}
and summing (
79
) over
k
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\rho_{\mathbf{y}}^{k}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\right)\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}\rho_{\mathbf{y}}^{k-1-j}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{j},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{j},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{j},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{j}),\gamma))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\rho_{\mathbf{y}}}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\right)\frac{\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}}{1-\rho_{\mathbf{y}}}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2},

(80)

substituting (
80
) into (
78
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k+1}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8-4\alpha\mu_{g}}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\right)\frac{2\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}(4-2\alpha\mu_{g})}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8-4\alpha\mu_{g}}{\alpha^{3}\mu_{g}}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\right)\frac{2\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}(4-2\alpha\mu_{g})}{\alpha^{3}\mu_{g}}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2},

where the second inequality comes from
\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k+1}=\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k}-\alpha\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})
.
∎

Lemma C.6
.

Under Assumptions
2.1
,
2.2
, for all
t\in[T]
, consider the sequences
\{\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}\}_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}
generated by Algorithm
3
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\beta\mu_{g}}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta\mu_{g}}\right)\frac{2(1+\lambda)L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}}{\beta\mu_{g}}\frac{8-4\alpha\mu_{g}}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta\mu_{g}}\right)\frac{2(1+\lambda)L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}}{\beta\mu_{g}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\right)\frac{2\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}(4-2\alpha\mu_{g})}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta\mu_{g}}\right)\left(1+\frac{1}{\lambda}\right)\frac{2\gamma^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}}{\beta\mu_{g}}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{v}}\widehat{\Phi}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8-4\beta\mu_{g}}{\beta^{3}\mu_{g}}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{16}{\beta\mu_{g}}-4\beta\mu_{g}\right)\frac{2(8-4\alpha\mu_{g})L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}}{\alpha\beta^{3}\mu_{g}^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}
