\displaystyle+\left(\frac{16}{\beta\mu_{g}}-4\beta\mu_{g}\right)\left(\frac{16}{\alpha\mu_{g}}-4\alpha\mu_{g}\right)\frac{2\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}}{\alpha\beta^{3}\mu_{g}^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{16}{\beta\mu_{g}}-4\beta\mu_{g}\right)\frac{2\gamma^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}}{\beta^{3}\mu_{g}}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2}.

Proof.

Let
\beta\leq\frac{1}{L_{g,1}}
, due to the strongly convexity of
\widehat{\Phi}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\cdot)
we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k+1}-\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\beta\mu_{g}\right)\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}-\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k+1}-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\left(4-2\beta\mu_{g}\right)\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}-\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})\right\|^{2},

(81)

additionally, it follows that

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle(1+\theta)\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k-1},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k-1})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{1}{\theta}\right)\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})-\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k-1},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+\theta)(1-\beta\mu_{g})\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k-1},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k-1})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{1}{\theta}\right)(1+\lambda)\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})-\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{1}{\theta}\right)\left(1+\frac{1}{\lambda}\right)\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k-1})-\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k-1},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(i)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\beta\mu_{g}}{2}\right)\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k-1},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k-1})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k-1}\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta\mu_{g}}\right)(1+\lambda)L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k}-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta\mu_{g}}\right)\left(1+\frac{1}{\lambda}\right)L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k}-\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k-1}\right\|^{2}

where the last inequality comes from Lemma
C.4
, then define
\rho_{\mathbf{v}}:=1-\frac{\beta\mu_{g}}{2}
and substitute the update rule of
\mathbf{x}
and
\mathbf{y}
shown in (
91
), after telescoping we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\rho_{\mathbf{v}}^{k}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta\mu_{g}}\right)(1+\lambda)\alpha^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}\rho_{\mathbf{v}}^{k-1-j}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{j},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{j})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta\mu_{g}}\right)\left(1+\frac{1}{\lambda}\right)\gamma^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}\rho_{\mathbf{v}}^{k-1-j}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{j},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{j},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{j},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{j}),\gamma))\right\|^{2},

(82)

and summing (
82
) over
k
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1{-}\rho_{\mathbf{v}}}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta\mu_{g}}\right)\frac{(1{+}\lambda)\alpha^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}}{1-\rho_{\mathbf{v}}}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta\mu_{g}}\right)\left(1+\frac{1}{\lambda}\right)\frac{\gamma^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}}{1-\rho_{\mathbf{v}}}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2}.

(83)

Remind of Lemma
C.5
, substitute its first inequality into (
83
), we then obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\beta\mu_{g}}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta\mu_{g}}\right)\frac{2(1+\lambda)\alpha^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}}{\beta\mu_{g}}\frac{8-4\alpha\mu_{g}}{\alpha^{3}\mu_{g}}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta\mu_{g}}\right)\frac{2(1+\lambda)\alpha^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}}{\beta\mu_{g}}\left(1+\frac{2}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\right)\frac{2\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}(4-2\alpha\mu_{g})}{\alpha^{3}\mu_{g}}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta\mu_{g}}\right)\left(1+\frac{1}{\lambda}\right)\frac{2\gamma^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}}{\beta\mu_{g}}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2},

(84)

finally substituting (
84
) into (
81
), it implies that

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k+1}{-}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8{-}4\beta\mu_{g}}{\beta\mu_{g}}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{16}{\beta\mu_{g}}-4\beta\mu_{g}\right)\frac{(1{+}\lambda)L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}}{\beta\mu_{g}}\frac{8{-}4\alpha\mu_{g}}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle{+}\left(\frac{16}{\beta\mu_{g}}{-}4\beta\mu_{g}\right)\frac{(1{+}\lambda)L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}}{\beta\mu_{g}}\left(\frac{16}{\alpha\mu_{g}}{-}4\alpha\mu_{g}\right)\frac{\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}}{\alpha\mu_{g}}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle{+}\left(\frac{16}{\beta\mu_{g}}-4\beta\mu_{g}\right)\left(1+\frac{1}{\lambda}\right)\frac{\gamma^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}}{\beta\mu_{g}}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2},

(85)

let
\lambda=1
, and substitute
\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k+1}=\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}-\beta\nabla_{\mathbf{v}}\widehat{\Phi}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k})
into (
85
), the proof is finished by

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{v}}\widehat{\Phi}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8-4\beta\mu_{g}}{\beta^{3}\mu_{g}}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{16}{\beta\mu_{g}}-4\beta\mu_{g}\right)\frac{2(8-4\alpha\mu_{g})L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}}{\alpha\beta^{3}\mu_{g}^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{16}{\beta\mu_{g}}-4\beta\mu_{g}\right)\left(\frac{16}{\alpha\mu_{g}}-4\alpha\mu_{g}\right)\frac{2\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}}{\alpha\beta^{3}\mu_{g}^{2}}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{16}{\beta\mu_{g}}-4\beta\mu_{g}\right)\frac{2\gamma^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}}{\beta^{3}\mu_{g}}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2}.

∎

C.2
Proof of Theorem
4.3

Theorem C.7

(Restatement of Theorem
4.3
)
.

Under Assumptions
2.1
-
2.3
and
4.2
, let
\beta=\frac{1}{L_{g,1}}
,
\alpha=\frac{1}{L_{g,1}}
and
\gamma\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{4L_{F}},\frac{1}{456\kappa_{g}^{4}L_{g,1}L_{\mathbf{v}}\sqrt{\kappa_{F}}}\right\}
, Algorithm
3
can obtain

\displaystyle\mathrm{Reg}_{w}(T)\leq\left(8\delta^{2}+4(1+\eta^{w})^{2}L_{f,1}^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}D^{2}+2(1+\eta^{w})^{2}L_{f,0}^{2}\right)\frac{T}{W^{2}},

with
L_{F}
,
L_{\mathbf{v}}
and
\kappa_{F}
are some constants, the total number of inner iterations
\mathcal{I}_{T}
satisfies

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{T}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8QW^{2}T}{\gamma\delta^{2}}+(3\kappa_{F}\kappa_{g}L_{g,1}^{2}+1024\kappa_{g}^{5}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2})\left(\frac{8(1+\eta^{w})^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}D^{2}}{\delta^{2}\mu_{g}^{2}}+\frac{8}{\mu_{g}^{2}\kappa_{F}}\right)T

\displaystyle+24\kappa_{g}^{3}L_{g,1}^{2}\left(\frac{8(1+\eta^{w})L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}}{\delta^{2}\mu_{g}^{4}}+\frac{1}{L_{g,1}^{2}}\right)T.

Proof.

It begins with

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\eta\nabla\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))+\frac{1}{W}\left(\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\eta^{w}\nabla f_{t-w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))\right),\gamma)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(i)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\eta\nabla\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma)\right\|+\frac{1}{W}\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\eta^{w}\nabla f_{t-w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))\right\|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma)\right\|^{2}+\frac{2}{W^{2}}\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\eta^{w}\nabla f_{t-w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(ii)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma)\right\|^{2}+\frac{2(1+\eta^{w})^{2}L_{f,0}^{2}}{W^{2}},

(86)

where
(i)
comes from Lemma
C.1
and
(ii)
comes from the
L_{f,0}
-Lipschitz Continuity of
f_{t}(\cdot)
in Assumption
2.2
. Then

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))+\nabla\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\nabla\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(i)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma)\right\|+\left\|\nabla\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\nabla\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))\right\|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma)\right\|^{2}+2L_{f,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})-\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(ii)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma)\right\|^{2}+\frac{2(1+\eta^{w})^{2}L_{f,1}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}D^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}W^{2}},

(87)

where
(i)
comes from Lemma
C.1
and
(ii)
from Lemma
C.2
, substituting (
87
) into (
86
) to get

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma)\right\|^{2}+\frac{4(1+\eta^{w})^{2}L_{f,1}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}D^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}W^{2}}+\frac{2(1+\eta^{w})^{2}L_{f,0}^{2}}{W^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t},\mathbf{v}_{t}),\gamma)\right\|^{2}+8\left\|\nabla\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t},\mathbf{v}_{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{4(1+\eta^{w})^{2}L_{f,1}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}D^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}W^{2}}+\frac{2(1+\eta^{w})^{2}L_{f,0}^{2}}{W^{2}}.

(88)

We also have

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t},\mathbf{v}_{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}\widehat{g}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))\mathbf{v}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}\widehat{g}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})\mathbf{v}_{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2L_{f,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})-\mathbf{y}_{t}\right\|^{2}+4L_{g,2}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))\right\|^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})-\mathbf{y}_{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+4L_{g,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t-1.w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\mathbf{v}_{t}\right\|^{2},

note that due to Lemma
B.3
, we still have
\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\cdot)\right\|\leq\frac{L_{f,0}}{\mu_{g}}
for all
t\in[T]
, and then it follows that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t},\mathbf{v}_{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2L_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{4L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,2}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})-\mathbf{y}_{t}\right\|^{2}+8L_{g,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\mathbf{v}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+8L_{g,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})-\mathbf{v}_{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(i)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(2L_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{4L_{g,1}^{2}L_{g,2}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}+\frac{16L_{f,1}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}+\frac{16L_{f,0}^{2}L_{f,1}^{2}L_{g,2}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{4}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})-\mathbf{y}_{t}\right\|^{2}+8L_{g,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})-\mathbf{v}_{t}\right\|^{2},

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle\kappa_{F}\mu_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})-\mathbf{y}_{t}\right\|^{2}+8L_{g,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})-\mathbf{v}_{t}\right\|^{2},

(89)

where
(i)
comes from

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\mathbf{v}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})\right\|^{2}\leq\left(\frac{2L_{f,1}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}+\frac{2L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,2}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{4}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}-\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|^{2},

as the similar result in (
32
),
\kappa_{F}
is defined in (
47
).

Substituting (
89
) into (
87
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\left\|\nabla\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t},\mathbf{v}_{t})\right\|^{2}+2\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t},\mathbf{v}_{t}),\gamma)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\kappa_{F}\mu_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})-\mathbf{y}_{t}\right\|^{2}+16L_{g,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})-\mathbf{v}_{t}\right\|^{2}+2\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t},\mathbf{v}_{t}),\gamma)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(i)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2\kappa_{F}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})\right\|^{2}+16\kappa_{g}^{2}\left\|\nabla\widehat{\Phi}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t})\right\|^{2}+2\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t},\mathbf{v}_{t}),\gamma)\right\|^{2}\overset{(ii)}{\leq}\frac{2\delta^{2}}{W^{2}},

(90)

where
(i)
comes from the strongly convexity of
\widehat{g}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x},\cdot)
and
\widehat{\Phi}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\cdot)
and
(ii)
comes from the stop condition defined in (
18
). Finally, substituting (
90
) into (
88
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla\widehat{f}_{t-1,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma)\right\|^{2}+\frac{4(1+\eta^{w})^{2}L_{f,1}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}D^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}W^{2}}+\frac{2(1+\eta^{w})^{2}L_{f,0}^{2}}{W^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8\delta^{2}}{W^{2}}+\frac{4(1+\eta^{w})^{2}L_{f,1}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}D^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}W^{2}}+\frac{2(1+\eta^{w})^{2}L_{f,0}^{2}}{W^{2}},

and thus

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\nabla\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})),\gamma)\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\left(8\delta^{2}+4(1+\eta^{w})^{2}L_{f,1}^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}D^{2}+2(1+\eta^{w})^{2}L_{f,0}^{2}\right)\frac{T}{W^{2}}.

Then, we prove the upper bound of the inner iteration count
\mathcal{I}_{T}
over the total
T
rounds. Consider in step
t
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}=\mathbf{y}_{t},

\displaystyle\quad\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k+1}\leftarrow\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k}-\alpha\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})

\displaystyle\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}=\mathbf{v}_{t},

\displaystyle\quad\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k+1}\leftarrow\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}-\beta\nabla_{\mathbf{v}}\widehat{\Phi}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k})

\displaystyle\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0}=\mathbf{x}_{t},

\displaystyle\quad\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k+1}\leftarrow\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k}-\gamma\nabla\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k})\right),

(91)

following the similarity proof process in Lemma
B.8
, it holds that

\displaystyle\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}^{+},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{+}))-\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x})),\mathbf{x}^{+}-\mathbf{x}\right\rangle+\frac{L_{F}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{+}-\mathbf{x}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\gamma\left\langle\nabla\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x})),\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{v}),\gamma)\right\rangle+\frac{\gamma^{2}L_{F}}{2}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{v}),\gamma)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\gamma\left\langle\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{v}),\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{v}),\gamma)\right\rangle+\frac{\gamma^{2}L_{F}}{2}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{v}),\gamma)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\gamma\left\langle\nabla\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))-\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{v}),\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{v}),\gamma)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\left(\gamma-\frac{\gamma^{2}L_{F}}{2}-\frac{\gamma}{2}\right)\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{v}),\gamma)\right\|^{2}+\frac{\gamma}{2}\left\|\nabla\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))-\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{v})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{\gamma}{2}-\frac{\gamma^{2}L_{F}}{2}\right)\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y},\mathbf{v}),\gamma)\right\|^{2}+\frac{\gamma L_{F}\mu_{g}^{2}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x})-\mathbf{y}\right\|^{2}+4\gamma L_{g,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})-\mathbf{v}\right\|^{2},

thus after some rearranging and summing over
k=0,\dots,K_{t}-1
, we have

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\gamma}{2}-\frac{\gamma^{2}L_{F}}{2}\right)\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t})){-}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t+1},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t+1})){+}\frac{\gamma\kappa_{F}\mu_{g}^{2}}{2}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k}){-}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k}\right\|^{2}{+}4\gamma L_{g,1}^{2}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k}){-}\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}\right\|^{2}.

(92)

Now, in Lemma
C.5
and
C.6
, let
\alpha=\frac{1}{L_{g,1}}
,
\beta=\frac{1}{L_{g,1}}
, thus
\alpha\mu_{g}=\frac{1}{\kappa_{g}}
,
\beta\mu_{g}=\frac{1}{\kappa_{g}}
, it implies that

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k}\right\|^{2}<

\displaystyle 2\kappa_{g}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}+8\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{4}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2}

(93)

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})\right\|^{2}<

\displaystyle 8\kappa_{g}L_{g,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}+32\gamma^{2}\kappa_{g}^{4}L_{g,1}^{2}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2}

(94)

and

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}\right\|^{2}<

\displaystyle 2\kappa_{g}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}+128\kappa_{g}^{3}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+(512\kappa_{g}^{6}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}+16\kappa_{g}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2})\gamma^{2}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2}

(95)

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{v}}\widehat{\Phi}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k})\right\|^{2}<

\displaystyle 8\kappa_{g}L_{g,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}+256\kappa_{g}^{3}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+(512\kappa_{g}^{6}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}+32\kappa_{g}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2})\gamma^{2}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2}.

(96)

Substituting (
93
) and (
95
) into (
92
), we obtain

\displaystyle\gamma\left(\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\gamma L_{F}}{2}-(4\kappa_{F}\kappa_{g}^{4}\mu_{g}^{2}+2048\kappa_{g}^{6}L_{g,1}^{4}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}+64\kappa_{g}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2})\gamma^{2}\right)\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t+1},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t+1}))

\displaystyle+(\kappa_{F}\kappa_{g}\mu_{g}^{2}+512\kappa_{g}^{3}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2})\gamma\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\right\|+8\gamma\kappa_{g}L_{g,1}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2},

(97)

let (
94
)
\times\gamma\kappa_{F}\cdot\frac{1}{4}
and (
96
)
\times 8\gamma\kappa_{g}^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{4}
plus (
97
) to get

\displaystyle\gamma\left(\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\gamma L_{F}}{2}-(4\kappa_{F}\kappa_{g}^{4}\mu_{g}^{2}+2048\kappa_{g}^{6}L_{g,1}^{4}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}+64\kappa_{g}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2})\gamma^{2}\right)\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\gamma\kappa_{F}}{4}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\nabla\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})\right\|^{2}+2\gamma\kappa_{g}^{2}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{v}}\widehat{\Phi}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))-\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t+1},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t+1}))+\gamma(8\kappa_{g}L_{g,1}^{2}+16\kappa_{g}^{3}L_{g,1}^{2})\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\gamma(\kappa_{F}\kappa_{g}\mu_{g}^{2}+512\kappa_{g}^{3}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}+2\kappa_{F}\kappa_{g}L_{g,1}^{2}+512\kappa_{g}^{5}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2})\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle+\gamma^{3}(1024\kappa_{g}^{8}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}+64\kappa_{g}^{4}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}+8\kappa_{F}\kappa_{g}^{4}L_{g,1}^{2})\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2},

to ensure

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}{-}\frac{\gamma L_{F}}{2}{-}(4\kappa_{F}\kappa_{g}^{4}\mu_{g}^{2}{+}2048\kappa_{g}^{6}L_{g,1}^{4}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}{+}64\kappa_{g}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2})\gamma^{2}{-}8\gamma^{2}\kappa_{F}\kappa_{g}^{4}L_{g,1}^{2}{-}\gamma^{2}(1024\kappa_{g}^{8}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}{+}64\kappa_{g}^{4}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2})\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\gamma L_{F}}{2}-(12\kappa_{F}\kappa_{g}^{4}L_{g,1}^{2}+2112\kappa_{g}^{6}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}+1080\kappa_{g}^{8}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2})\gamma^{2}\geq

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\gamma L_{F}}{2}-3204\kappa_{F}\kappa_{g}^{8}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}\gamma^{2}\geq

\displaystyle 0

with
\sqrt{3204}<57
, we can set the following condition to satisfy the above inequality,

\displaystyle\gamma\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{4L_{F}},\frac{1}{456\kappa_{g}^{4}L_{g,1}L_{\mathbf{v}}\sqrt{\kappa_{F}}}\right\}.

Finally, it follows that

\displaystyle\frac{\delta^{2}K_{t}}{4W^{2}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\mathcal{G}_{\mathcal{X}}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\widetilde{\nabla}\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k}),\gamma))\right\|^{2}{+}\frac{\kappa_{F}}{4}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\nabla\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k})\right\|^{2}{+}2\kappa_{g}^{2}\sum_{k=0}^{K_{t}-1}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{v}}\widehat{\Phi}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{k},\mathbf{v}_{t}^{k})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2Q}{\gamma}+(\kappa_{F}\kappa_{g}\mu_{g}^{2}+512\kappa_{g}^{3}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2}+2\kappa_{F}\kappa_{g}L_{g,1}^{2}+512\kappa_{g}^{5}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2})\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle+(8\kappa_{g}L_{g,1}^{2}+16\kappa_{g}^{3}L_{g,1}^{2})\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2},

(98)

we also have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle 2\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1}^{K_{t-1}})-\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1}^{K_{t-1}})\right\|^{2}+2\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1}^{K_{t-1}})-\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{K_{t-1}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2(1+\eta^{w})^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}D^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}W^{2}}+\frac{2}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}\widehat{g}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1}^{K_{t-1}},\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{K_{t-1}})\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{2(1+\eta^{w})^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}D^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}W^{2}}+\frac{2}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\frac{\delta^{2}}{W^{2}\kappa_{F}},

(99)

where the second inequality comes from Lemma
C.2
, similarily,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{0},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{0})-\mathbf{v}_{t}^{0}\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle 2\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1}^{K_{t-1}},\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{K_{t-1}}){-}\mathbf{v}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1}^{K_{t-1}},\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{K_{t-1}})\right\|^{2}+2\left\|\mathbf{v}_{t-1,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t-1}^{K_{t-1}},\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{K_{t-1}}){-}\mathbf{v}_{t-1}^{K_{t-1}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2(1+\eta^{w})L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{4}W^{2}}+\frac{2}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\frac{\delta^{2}}{8W^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}},

(100)

where the second inequality comes from Lemma
C.3
.

Substituting (
99
) and (
100
) into (
98
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{\delta^{2}}{4W^{2}}K_{t}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2Q}{\gamma}+(3\kappa_{F}\kappa_{g}L_{g,1}^{2}+1024\kappa_{g}^{5}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2})\left(\frac{2(1+\eta^{w})^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}D^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}W^{2}}+\frac{2}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\frac{\delta^{2}}{W^{2}\kappa_{F}}\right)

\displaystyle+24\kappa_{g}^{3}L_{g,1}^{2}\left(\frac{2(1+\eta^{w})L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{4}W^{2}}+\frac{2}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\frac{\delta^{2}}{8W^{2}\kappa_{g}^{2}}\right),

thus the proof is finished by

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{T}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8QW^{2}T}{\gamma\delta^{2}}+(3\kappa_{F}\kappa_{g}L_{g,1}^{2}+1024\kappa_{g}^{5}L_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mathbf{v}}^{2})\left(\frac{8(1+\eta^{w})^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}D^{2}}{\delta^{2}\mu_{g}^{2}}+\frac{8}{\mu_{g}^{2}\kappa_{F}}\right)T

\displaystyle+24\kappa_{g}^{3}L_{g,1}^{2}\left(\frac{8(1+\eta^{w})L_{f,0}^{2}L_{g,1}^{2}}{\delta^{2}\mu_{g}^{4}}+\frac{1}{L_{g,1}^{2}}\right)T.

∎

C.3
Proof of Theorem
4.4

Theorem C.8

(Restatement of Theorem
4.4
)
.

Consider any online hypergradient-based algorithm, under Assumptions
2.1
-
2.3
and
4.2
with
d_{1}=d_{2}\geq\Omega(T)
, given window size
w>0
and
\eta\in(0,1)
, there exist
\{f_{t},g_{t}\}_{t=1}^{T}
for which
\mathrm{Reg}_{w}(T)\geq\Omega(T/W^{2})
.

Proof.

Constructing a set of functions with linear variation is simple, for example, consider that we have
F_{t}(\mathbf{x})=\widetilde{F}([\mathbf{x}]_{t})
, it naturally holds that

\displaystyle V_{T}=\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sup_{\mathbf{x}}|F_{t}(\mathbf{x})-F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x})|=\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sup_{\mathbf{x}}\left|\widetilde{F}([\mathbf{x}]_{t})-\widetilde{F}([\mathbf{x}]_{t-1})\right|=(T-1)\left(\max_{z\in\mathbb{R}}\widetilde{F}(z)-\min_{z\in\mathbb{R}}\widetilde{F}(z)\right)=\Theta(T),

the function
F_{t}(\mathbf{x})
requires the algorithm to achieve the smallest possible value at a new coordinate at each time step
t
, therefore, unless the algorithm knows the function at the next
t
a prior,
[\mathbf{x}_{t}]_{t}=0
holds and the regret
\left\|\nabla\widetilde{F}(0)\right\|
is unavoidable.

Step 1: Constructing linearly changing
f_{t}
that satisfying Assumptions
2.2
,
2.3
.

Specifically, consider the upper-level objective function as follows:

\displaystyle f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=

\displaystyle c\sigma([\mathbf{y}]_{t})+c\sigma([\mathbf{x}]_{t}),

where
\sigma(\cdot)
denotes the sigmoid function.
For any
\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
and
\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right\|\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}c}{4},\quad\left\|\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})-\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}^{\prime})\right\|\leq\frac{\sqrt{3}c}{18}\left\|(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})-(\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}^{\prime})\right\|,

thus we set
c=\min\{\frac{Q}{2},2\sqrt{2}L_{f,0},6\sqrt{3}L_{f,1}\}
to let
f_{t}
satisfies Assumptions
2.2
and
2.3
for all
t\in[T]
.

Step 2: Constructing
g_{t}
that satisfying Assumptions
2.1
,
2.2
and
4.2
.

We define a simple inner-level objective function as follows:

\displaystyle g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\equiv g(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=\frac{\mu}{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}-\vec{\phi}(\mathbf{x})\right\|^{2},

where
\left[\vec{\phi}(\mathbf{x})\right]_{i}=\sigma([\mathbf{x}]_{i})/\sqrt{d}
.
For any given
w>0
and
\eta\in(0,1)
, it is true that

\displaystyle\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x})=\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x})=\mathbf{y}_{t-1}^{*}(\mathbf{x})=\cdots=\mathbf{y}_{t-w+1}^{*}(\mathbf{x})=\vec{\phi}(\mathbf{x}).

For any
\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,
\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=\mu(\mathbf{y}-\vec{\phi}(\mathbf{x}))
, it follows that

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=\mu\mathbf{I}_{d},\quad\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=\begin{bmatrix}-\frac{\mu}{\sqrt{d}}\sigma^{\prime}([\mathbf{x}]_{1})\\
&\ddots\\
&&-\frac{\mu}{\sqrt{d}}\sigma^{\prime}([\mathbf{x}]_{d})\end{bmatrix},

g_{t}(\cdot)
is
\mu_{g}
-strongly convex if
\mu\geq\mu_{g}
, and
\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right\|\leq\frac{\mu}{4\sqrt{d}}<\frac{\mu}{4}\leq L_{g,1}
if set
\mu\leq 4L_{g,1}
, thus
\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})
is
L_{g,1}
-Lipschitz continuous.

\nabla_{\mathbf{y}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\cdot)
satisfy
L_{g,2}
-Lipschitz continuity because it is a constant matrix.
For any
\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}^{\prime}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}^{\prime})\right\|\leq\frac{\sqrt{3}\mu}{18\sqrt{d}}\left\|(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})-(\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}^{\prime})\right\|,

and
\nabla_{\mathbf{x}\mathbf{y}}^{2}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})
is
L_{g,2}
-Lipschitz continuous if
\mu\leq 6\sqrt{3d}L_{g,2}
.
We set
\mu=\min\{4L_{g,1},6\sqrt{3d}L_{g,2}\}
to let
g_{t}
satisfies Assumptions
2.1
and
2.2
for all
t\in[T]
.

Also note that
\left\|\vec{\phi}(\mathbf{x})\right\|\leq\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{d}1/d}=1
, thus
\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x})\in\mathcal{Y}=\{\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\,|\,\left\|\mathbf{y}\right\|\leq D\}
with
D=1
, Assumption
4.2
is satisfied.

Step 3: Characterizing the Lower Bound of Window-Averaged Regret.

It holds that

\displaystyle F_{t}(\mathbf{x})=

\displaystyle f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))=c\sigma(\frac{\sigma([\mathbf{x}]_{t})}{\sqrt{d}})+c\sigma([\mathbf{x}]_{t})

\displaystyle[\nabla f_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{t}^{*}(\mathbf{x}))]_{i}=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}c\sigma^{\prime}(\frac{\sigma([\mathbf{x}]_{i})}{\sqrt{d}})\frac{\sigma^{\prime}([\mathbf{x}]_{i})}{\sqrt{d}}+c\sigma^{\prime}([\mathbf{x}]_{i})>c\sigma^{\prime}([\mathbf{x}]_{i})\quad&\text{if }i=t\\
0&\text{otherwise}\end{cases},

then with
[\mathbf{x}_{t}]_{t}=0
,
\left\|\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\right\|>c\sigma^{\prime}(0)=\frac{c}{4}
, we finally have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Reg}_{w}(T)=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\nabla\widehat{f}_{t,w}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\mathbf{y}_{t,w}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{t}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\frac{1}{W}\sum_{i=0}^{w-1}\eta^{i}\nabla f_{t-i}(\mathbf{x}_{t},\vec{\phi}(\mathbf{x}))\right\|^{2}>\frac{1}{W^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\frac{c}{4}e_{t}+c\sum_{i=1}^{w-1}\eta^{i}\sigma^{\prime}([\mathbf{x}_{t}]_{t-i})e_{t-i}\right\|^{2}\geq\frac{c^{2}T}{16W^{2}}.

Thus we finish the proof.
∎
